# Veteran: original English description comparison

[繁體中文](../LOCALIZATION_COMPARISON.md) | [Player descriptions](README.md) | [Technical index](SOURCE_INDEX.md)

Implementation: Release 1.13.1, fixed SHA `7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e`. Original English: Steam Build `25606770`, extracted 2026-10-02, `content/localization/ui` (resource hash `63564edcb7c3c5ee`), `en` (code `0`). English UI export SHA-256: `fb54bb69880e08b3a6e1c359bd970ed2416748bf5513d45c4b8a39c545e8c668`.

Comparisons evaluate behavior and meaning. A missing detail is **Not covered by the description**, not an error. Only explicit English contradictions receive a player-page erratum. The allowed results are Consistent, Explicit contradiction, Not covered by the description, No corresponding implementation evidence found, and Cannot confirm. Static template substitution is identified as reconstruction; it is not an observed game screen. Chinese comparison verdicts and statistics are not used as English conclusions.

## Demolition Stockpile

Original name and full raw description template, all placeholder/name hashes and formatting derivation are preserved in [source evidence](veteran_replenish_grenades.md#original-english-template-and-reconstruction). Display-name hash: `4762b23c`. Every row below references description key `loc_talent_veteran_grenade_regeneration_per_grenade_desc`, hash `3b1bc587`, in the English UI resource specified above.

| Rule | Original game English and resource / key / hash | Code behavior and fixed source / method / lines | Result | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|

[Interval and amount settings](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/talent/talent_settings_veteran.lua#L141-L147); [Grenade replenishment buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L1813-L1911) | Consistent | The referenced parameter and actual call both restore one grenade to the owner. |

[Talent definition and display parameters](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/ability/archetype_talents/talents/veteran_talents.lua#L1988-L2030); [Interval and amount settings](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/talent/talent_settings_veteran.lua#L141-L147); [Grenade replenishment buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L1813-L1911) | Consistent | The localized variant names, units and selected interval parameters match the fixed-loadout implementation; `time = 75` is unused in the template. |

| Throwing again, strict deadline and repeated intervals | The template says `at set intervals` without reset, equality or catch-up rules; same resource/key/hash | Existing `next_grenade_t` is preserved; grant requires `next_grenade_t < t`, clears the deadline and restores at most once per update. | Restoration bonuses and capacity | The template does not describe grant scaling or maximum capacity; same resource/key/hash | `restore_ability_charge` uses `ignore_stat_buffs = true`; resource and charge counts are clamped; extra grenade capacity still applies. [Charge restoration and resource limits](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/ability/player_unit_ability_extension.lua#L1105-L1179); [Remaining, missing and maximum charges](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/ability/player_unit_ability_extension.lua#L712-L770); [Grenade ability configuration](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/ability/player_abilities/abilities/veteran_abilities.lua#L27-L62); [Resource cost per charge](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/ability/player_unit_ability_extension.lua#L1437-L1461) | Not covered by the description | An omitted settlement rule is not an explicit contradiction. ## Precision Strikes

Original name: `Precision Strikes`, key `loc_talent_veteran_increased_weakspot_damage`, hash `c82bfb26`. Full raw description: `{damage:%s} Weak Spot Damage.`, key `loc_talent_veteran_increased_weakspot_damage_desc`, hash `8c42b6fb`. All strings are from the English UI resource specified above. [Template and formatting reconstruction](veteran_increased_weakspot_damage.md#original-english-template-and-reconstruction) preserves the exact wording and derives `+30% Weak Spot Damage.` statically.

| Rule | Original game English and resource / key / hash | Code behavior and fixed source / method / lines | Result | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Stat and magnitude | `{damage:%s} Weak Spot Damage.`; `ui / loc_talent_veteran_increased_weakspot_damage_desc / 8c42b6fb`; reconstructed `+30%` | Talent parameter and passive both use `weakspot_damage = 0.30`; neutral multiplier is `1`, and `Buff._calculate_stat_buffs` adds the bonus. [Talent definition and display parameter](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/ability/archetype_talents/talents/veteran_talents.lua#L1004-L1020); [Weakspot damage setting](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/talent/talent_settings_veteran.lua#L110-L112); [Passive buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L2115-L2121); [Stat type and neutral values](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/buff_settings.lua#L1025-L1059); [Buff._calculate_stat_buffs](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L681-L737) | Consistent | The stat and displayed magnitude match. The template does not specify whole-hit multiplication. |
| Weakspot requirement | Same full template and resource/key/hash; `Weak Spot Damage` | `_finesse_boost_damage` reads this stat only under `if hit_weakspot`, lines 717–724. [Shared _finesse_boost_damage](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/utilities/attack/damage_calculation.lua#L672-L783) | Consistent | The identified damage condition matches the implementation. |
| Component and existing bonuses | The template does not explain a base-plus-finesse formula; same resource/key/hash | `_finesse_boost_damage` combines applicable bonuses additively and multiplies `base_finesse_damage`; the caller adds it to non-finesse damage. [Shared _finesse_boost_damage](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/utilities/attack/damage_calculation.lua#L672-L783); [Damage stage and downstream ordering](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/utilities/attack/damage_calculation.lua#L60-L110) | Not covered by the description | It does not explicitly claim that the whole hit receives a fixed 30% increase. This settlement detail is omitted, not contradicted. |
| Melee, ranged and critical weakspot scope | The template gives no attack-type or critical combination rules; same resource/key/hash | The generic weakspot stat joins melee/ranged modifiers. Critical and weakspot boosts form one component; this talent contributes `0.30` once when `hit_weakspot` is true. [Shared _finesse_boost_damage](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/utilities/attack/damage_calculation.lua#L672-L783) | Not covered by the description | The short stat description does not exclude either attack type or promise two applications on a critical weakspot hit. |
| Profile, armor, hit zone and damage floor | The template gives no per-weapon or per-target formula; same resource/key/hash | `_finesse_boost_damage`, lines 674–710, derives the component using profile flags, armor boost, weakspot adjustment, critical boost, capped curve and floor. Later stages are separate. [Shared _finesse_boost_damage](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/utilities/attack/damage_calculation.lua#L672-L783); [Damage stage and downstream ordering](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/utilities/attack/damage_calculation.lua#L60-L110) | Not covered by the description | Actual whole-hit benefit needs the attack and target parameters; no universal weapon-specific benefit is claimed. |

## Exhilarating Takedown

Original name: `Exhilarating Takedown`, key `loc_talent_veteran_toughness_on_weakspot_kill`, hash `c6fc8551`. Every row uses description key `loc_talent_veteran_toughness_on_weakspot_kill_alt_desc`, hash `498dcee2`, from the English UI resource specified above. The [full template and reconstruction](veteran_replenish_toughness_on_weakspot_kill.md#original-english-template-and-reconstruction) preserve every placeholder and derive the values statically.

| Rule | Original game English and resource / key / hash | Code behavior and fixed source / method / lines | Result | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Trigger condition | `on Ranged Weakspot Kill`; `ui / loc_talent_veteran_toughness_on_weakspot_kill_alt_desc / 498dcee2` | `on_ranged_weakspot_kills` requires weakspot hit, death, and ranged type or `count_as_ranged_attack`. [Recovery proc and spawned damage-reduction buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L1716-L1750); [CheckProcFunctions.on_ranged_kill](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/helper_functions/check_proc_functions.lua#L216-L224); [Weakspot and combined proc checks](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/helper_functions/check_proc_functions.lua#L473-L520) | Consistent | The described trigger agrees with the event predicate. Exact special-weapon event production needs its own tracing. |
| Recovery parameter | `Replenish {toughness:%s} Toughness`; same resource/key/hash; reconstructed `15%` | Parameter and `replenish_percentage` use `0.15`. [Talent and display parameters](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/ability/archetype_talents/talents/veteran_talents.lua#L2302-L2340); [Recovery, duration and stack settings](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/talent/talent_settings_veteran.lua#L121-L126); [Recovery proc and spawned damage-reduction buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L1716-L1750) | Consistent | The displayed recovery percentage matches the implemented grant parameter. |
| Reduction, duration and effective cap | `gain {toughness_damage_reduction:%s} Toughness Damage Reduction for {duration:%s}s`; `Stacks {stacks:%s} times`; same resource/key/hash; reconstructed `+10%`, `8`, `3` | Spawned buff uses factor `0.9`, duration 8 and effective stat cap 3. The formatter derives `100 − 0.9 × 100 = 10`. [Talent and display parameters](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/ability/archetype_talents/talents/veteran_talents.lua#L2302-L2340); [Recovery, duration and stack settings](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/talent/talent_settings_veteran.lua#L121-L126); [Recovery proc and spawned damage-reduction buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L1716-L1750); [Effective stat-stack count](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L404-L410) | Consistent | Per-stack reduction, time and effective stack limit agree. The internal 6-second name string is not the localized display parameter. |
| Decay one at a time | `Stacks Decay one at a time.`; same resource/key/hash | `_remove_buff` removes one effective stack, resets the timer when removing from two or three, and deletes the last stack on its expiry. [Stack removal, timer reset and index removal](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buff_extension_base.lua#L635-L698); [Start-time reset clears expiry](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L619-L639); [Strict expiry test](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L29-L44) | Consistent | The explicit decay behavior agrees with the traced normal stack sequence. |
| Recovery basis, bonuses, cap and permissions | Template does not state maximum-versus-deficit basis or recovery restrictions; same resource/key/hash | `recover_percentage_toughness` uses maximum toughness and recovery multipliers, limits self-recovery by the deficit, and can reject recovery. [PlayerUnitToughnessExtension.max_toughness](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/toughness/player_unit_toughness_extension.lua#L79-L90); [Percentage recovery and clamps](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/toughness/player_unit_toughness_extension.lua#L253-L285); [Recovery permissions and restrictions](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/toughness/player_unit_toughness_extension.lua#L411-L475) | Not covered by the description | Those rules supplement the 15% parameter without contradicting it. |
| Multiplicative stack and other reduction settlement | Template does not state how stacked percentages or other reductions combine; same resource/key/hash | Stat aggregation multiplies `0.9` per effective stack; toughness settlement combines other factors before clamps and health spillover. [Toughness damage multiplier type](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/buff_settings.lua#L1001-L1004); [Stat aggregation per effective stack](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L689-L727); [Player toughness damage settlement](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/utilities/attack/damage_taken_calculation.lua#L211-L281) | Not covered by the description | Three effective stacks give 27.1% reduction. The text does not explicitly claim an additive 30% total or the same reduction to health damage. |
| Timer refresh, full Toughness and overflow counter | Template does not specify full-Toughness proc behavior, refresh rules or internal counters; same resource/key/hash | Proc adds the buff without testing actual recovery. Adding stacks refreshes the shared timer; stat count is capped at 3 while the internal count may exceed it. [Recovery proc and spawned damage-reduction buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L1716-L1750); [Shared buff instance and timer refresh](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buff_extension_base.lua#L434-L488); [Hard-cap check](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buff_extension_base.lua#L560-L589); [Internal stack changes](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L453-L515); [Effective stat-stack count](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L404-L410) | Not covered by the description | These implementation details are omissions; internal overflow provides no additional effective reduction. |

<a id="veteran_snipers_focus"></a>

## Marksman's Focus

The full raw template and parameter reconstruction are preserved in [source evidence](veteran_snipers_focus.md#original-english-template-and-reconstruction). Name key `loc_talent_veteran_snipers_focus`, hash `90f993d2`. Each rule below uses the English UI resource identified above, description key `loc_talent_veteran_snipers_focus_duration_desc`, hash `bd7cf7c2`.

| Rule | Original game English and resource / key / hash | Code behavior and fixed source / method / lines | Result | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Kill grant and base effective cap | `Ranged Weakspot kills grant {stacks:%s} stack(s) of Focus. Up to {max_stacks:%s} Max Stacks.`; `ui / loc_talent_veteran_snipers_focus_duration_desc / bd7cf7c2`; reconstructed `3 / 10` | `proc_func` requires weakspot, death and strict ranged type, then attempts three adds; `max_stacks = 10` caps applied stats. [Focus proc and stat cap](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L2755-L2883); [Effective stack count](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L404-L410). | Consistent | The grant condition, amount and base effective cap match the reconstructed description. |
| Five-second shared duration | `Lasts {duration:%s}s.`; same resource/key/hash; reconstructed `5s` | Stat buff duration is 5; expiry tests `t > start_time + 5`. [Core stat buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L2803-L2883); [Strict expiry](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L29-L44). | Consistent | The configured duration matches. Ordinary update delay does not contradict the five-second interval. |
| Weakspot-hit refresh | `Stacks are refreshed on Weakspot Hit`; same resource/key/hash | The non-grant weakspot branch refreshes existing stacks; it does not exclude melee. Qualifying ranged kills refresh through stack addition or the dedicated hard-cap refresh before rejection. [Proc branches](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L2755-L2802); [Explicit refresh and stack lookup](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buff_extension_base.lua#L592-L615); [Stack-add refresh](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buff_extension_base.lua#L434-L488); [Hard-cap refresh](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buff_extension_base.lua#L560-L589). | Consistent | Weakspot hits refresh active Focus. The text does not claim that nonlethal hits create new stacks. |
| One-at-a-time decay | `decay one at a time`; same resource/key/hash | Removing one in-cap stack refreshes the shared timer when stacks remain; last-stack expiry ends the effect. [Core timer flags](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L2803-L2883); [Removal and timer reset](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buff_extension_base.lua#L635-L698); [Reset clears expiry](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buffs/buff.lua#L619-L639). | Consistent | Effective Focus decays by one per approximately five-second interval without further refresh. |
| Per-stack ranged finesse and reload speed | `Each stack of Focus grants {power:%s} Ranged Finesse Strength, and {reload_speed:%s} Reload Speed.`; same resource/key/hash; reconstructed `+7.5% / +1%` | Per-stack values are `0.075` and `0.01`, both additive-multiplier stats applied per effective stack. [Ten-second flat Toughness buff](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/settings/buff/archetype_buff_templates/veteran_buff_templates.lua#L2311-L2327); [New instances without a stack cap](https://github.com/Aussiemon/Darktide-Source-Code/blob/7e662fcda16219d775b84af50322be2e9cd9d62e/scripts/extension_systems/buff/buff_extension_base.lua#L439-L484). | Not covered by the description | Overlapping instances each contribute 75 points and keep a separate duration. |
| Current Toughness on expiry | The template does not describe what happens when the enlarged maximum returns; `ui / loc_talent_veteran_combat_ability_increase_and_restore_toughness_to_coherency_description / b4e057ef` | The deficit is reduced by the maximum decrease, with a floor of zero. ## Comparison totals

Totals apply only to the 516 rules listed above, not to all Veteran talents: **231 Consistent**, **9 Explicit contradiction**, **267 Not covered by the description**, **0 No corresponding implementation evidence found**, **9 Cannot confirm**. Practiced Efficiency adds 7 independently judged rules; no explicit English contradiction is supported. Nine explicit English contradictions exist across eight talents: Focus Target! has one conditional-reset erratum; Covering Fire has one radius erratum; Leave No One Behind has one facing-condition erratum; Lock and Load has one rounding erratum; Enhanced Target Priority has one duration erratum; base Volley Fire has two numeric errata, and Marksman and Close Quarters Killzone each have one Infiltrate timing erratum. No English erratum is supported for the other 75 talents. Counter-Fire’s universal enemy coverage, Demolition Stockpile live loadout changes, Voice of Command’s literal all-enemy/all-state stagger outcome and Smoke Grenade’s independent enemy sight-range reduction remain unconfirmed. Catch a Breath’s intended scope of "target of a Melee Attack" for missed attempts remains unconfirmed. Shock Trooper’s intended enemy-contact scope of Critical Hits remains unconfirmed. Born Leader’s intended requested-versus-recovered wording basis remains unconfirmed. Duck and Dive’s full Dodging/Sprinting/Sliding event-producer coverage remains unconfirmed. Weapons Specialist’s intended kill-attribution meaning of Melee Kills/Ranged Kill remains unconfirmed. Survivalist’s exact team event ordering is unmeasured; no in-game execution or final game UI observation was performed.
